Measures:\n  A) Pruning curve: L2-norm based vision token pruning → hidden state degradation\n  B) Attention entropy per layer (text→vision attention distribution)\n\nUsage:\n    source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python analyze_openvla_oft.py\n\"\"\"\n\nimport glob\nimport json\nimport math\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\n\nimport h5py\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom PIL import Image\n\nDEVICE = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\nCHECKPOINT_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-oft-libero-object\"\nLIBERO_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/libero_data/datasets/libero_object\"\nN_VISION_TOKENS = 256\nIMAGES_PER_TASK = 10\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts\"\n\nKEEP_RATIOS = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0]\n\nTASK_PROMPTS = {\n    \"pick_up_the_alphabet_soup\": \"pick up the alphabet soup and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_cream_cheese\": \"pick up the cream cheese and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_butter\": \"pick up the butter and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_ketchup\": \"pick up the ketchup and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_tomato_sauce\": \"pick up the tomato sauce and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_bbq_sauce\": \"pick up the bbq sauce and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_chocolate_pudding\": \"pick up the chocolate pudding and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_salad_dressing\": \"pick up the salad dressing and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_milk\": \"pick up the milk and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_orange_juice\": \"pick up the orange juice and place it in the basket\",\n}\nDEFAULT_PROMPT = \"pick up the object and place it in the basket\"\n\n\ndef get_prompt_for_task(task_name):\n    tn_lower = task_name.lower()\n    for key, desc in TASK_PROMPTS.items():\n        if key in tn_lower:\n            return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {desc}?\\nOut:\"\n    return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {DEFAULT_PROMPT}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef load_libero_images():\n    hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"*.hdf5\")))\n    if not hdf5_files:\n        hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"**/*.hdf5\"), recursive=True))\n    print(f\"[*] Found {len(hdf5_files)} HDF5 files in {LIBERO_DIR}\")\n\n    all_images = []\n    task_names = []\n\n    for hdf5_path in hdf5_files:\n        task_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(hdf5_path))[0]\n\n        with h5py.File(hdf5_path, \"r\") as f:\n            if \"data\" not in f:\n                continue\n            demo_keys = sorted(\n                [k for k in f[\"data\"].keys() if k.startswith(\"demo\")],\n                key=lambda x: int(x.split(\"_\")[-1])\n            )\n            if not demo_keys:\n                continue\n\n            obs_keys = list(f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs\"].keys())\n            img_key = None\n            for candidate in [\"agentview_rgb\", \"agentview_image\", \"image\", \"rgb\"]:\n                if candidate in obs_keys:\n                    img_key = candidate\n                    break\n            if img_key is None:\n                for k in obs_keys:\n                    shape = f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs/{k}\"].shape\n                    if len(shape) == 4 and shape[-1] == 3:\n                        img_key = k\n                        break\n            if img_key is None:\n                continue\n\n            frame_pool = []\n            for dk in demo_keys:\n                n_frames = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"].shape[0]\n                frame_pool.append((dk, n_frames))\n            total_frames = sum(n for _, n in frame_pool)\n\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n            target = min(IMAGES_PER_TASK, total_frames)\n            indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, replace=False)\n            chosen.sort()\n\n            by_demo = {}\n            for idx in chosen:\n                dk, frame_i = indices[idx]\n                by_demo.setdefault(dk, []).append(frame_i)\n\n            sampled = 0\n            for dk, frame_indices in by_demo.items():\n                imgs = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"][:]\n                for fi in frame_indices:\n                    all_images.append(Image.fromarray(imgs[fi]))\n                    task_names.append(task_name)\n                    sampled += 1\n\n            print(f\"  {task_name}: {sampled} frames\")\n\n    unique_tasks = sorted(set(task_names))\n    print(f\"[*] Total: {len(all_images)} images from {len(unique_tasks)} tasks\")\n    return all_images, task_names, unique_tasks\n\n\ndef load_model():\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Loading OpenVLA-OFT from: {CHECKPOINT_PATH}\", flush=True)\n\n    # Add the checkpoint dir to path so trust_remote_code can find the modules\n    sys.path.insert(0, CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoConfig\n\n    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        CHECKPOINT_PATH,\n        config=config,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n        attn_implementation=\"eager\",\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n    ).to(DEVICE)\n    model.eval()\n\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n    # Load processor components\n    proc_module = __import__(\"processing_prismatic\", fromlist=[\"PrismaticImageProcessor\", \"PrismaticProcessor\"])\n    PrismaticImageProcessor = proc_module.PrismaticImageProcessor\n    PrismaticProcessor = proc_module.PrismaticProcessor\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    image_processor = PrismaticImageProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    processor = PrismaticProcessor(image_processor=image_processor, tokenizer=tokenizer)\n\n    print(f\"[*] Model loaded, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\", flush=True)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_bits(p, eps=1e-10):\n    p = p.clamp(min=eps)\n    return -(p * p.log2()).sum(dim=-1)\n\n\nclass ProjectorHook:\n    \"\"\"Hook to capture and optionally prune projector output.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        self.projected_features = None\n        self.norms = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.projected_features = output.detach().clone()\n        self.norms = output.detach().norm(dim=-1)  # (B, N)\n\n        if self.keep_ratio < 1.0:\n            B, N, D = output.shape\n            k = max(1, int(N * self.keep_ratio))\n            _, topk_idx = torch.topk(self.norms, k, dim=-1)\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=output.device, dtype=output.dtype)\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\n            output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n    n_images = len(images)\n    n_layers = model.language_model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    n_heads = model.language_model.config.num_attention_heads\n    print(f\"[*] LLM: {n_layers} layers, {n_heads} heads\", flush=True)\n\n    # Register projector hook\n    hook = ProjectorHook()\n    handle = model.projector.register_forward_hook(hook)\n\n    # === Part A: Attention Entropy ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part A: Attention Entropy Analysis\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    all_entropies = []\n    all_vision_attn_frac = []\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_attentions=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n\n        attentions = outputs.attentions\n\n        if idx == 0:\n            seq_len = attentions[0].shape[-1]\n            text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n            print(f\"[*] Seq len: {seq_len}, vision: [1, {1+N_VISION_TOKENS}), text starts: {text_start}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Attention shape: {attentions[0].shape}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Layers returned: {len(attentions)}\", flush=True)\n\n        text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n        sample_entropies = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n        sample_vis_frac = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n\n        for layer_idx in vis_sum = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            text_to_vision_norm = text_to_vision / vis_sum\n\n            ent = compute_entropy_bits(text_to_vision_norm)\n time\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ[\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\nos.environ[\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n\nimport h5py\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom PIL import Image\n\n# Use base OpenVLA code (OFT's modeling_prismatic.py has extra deps on prismatic package)\nBASE_MODEL_CODE_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-object\"\nsys.path.insert(0, BASE_MODEL_CODE_DIR)\nfrom configuration_prismatic import OpenVLAConfig\nfrom modeling_prismatic import OpenVLAForActionPrediction\nfrom processing_prismatic import PrismaticImageProcessor, PrismaticProcessor\n\nDEVICE = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\nCHECKPOINT_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-oft-libero-object\"\nLIBERO_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/libero_data/datasets/libero_object\"\nN_VISION_TOKENS = 256\nIMAGES_PER_TASK = 10\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts\"\n\nKEEP_RATIOS = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0]\n\nTASK_PROMPTS = {\n    \"pick_up_the_alphabet_soup\": \"pick up the alphabet soup and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_cream_cheese\": \"pick up the cream cheese and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_butter\": \"pick up the butter and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_ketchup\": \"pick up the ketchup and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_tomato_sauce\": \"pick up the tomato sauce and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_bbq_sauce\": \"pick up the bbq sauce and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_chocolate_pudding\": \"pick up the chocolate pudding and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_salad_dressing\": \"pick up the salad dressing and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_milk\": \"pick up the milk and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_orange_juice\": \"pick up the orange juice and place it in the basket\",\n}\nDEFAULT_PROMPT = \"pick up the object and place it in the basket\"\n\n\ndef get_prompt_for_task(task_name):\n    tn_lower = task_name.lower()\n    for key, desc in TASK_PROMPTS.items():\n        if key in tn_lower:\n            return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {desc}?\\nOut:\"\n    return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {DEFAULT_PROMPT}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef load_libero_images():\n    hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"*.hdf5\")))\n    if not hdf5_files:\n        hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"**/*.hdf5\"), recursive=True))\n    print(f\"[*] Found {len(hdf5_files)} HDF5 files in {LIBERO_DIR}\")\n\n    all_images = []\n    task_names = []\n\n    for hdf5_path in hdf5_files:\n        task_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(hdf5_path))[0]\n\n        with h5py.File(hdf5_path, \"r\") as f:\n            if \"data\" not in f:\n                continue\n            demo_keys = sorted(\n                [k for k in f[\"data\"].keys() if k.startswith(\"demo\")],\n                key=lambda x: int(x.split(\"_\")[-1])\n            )\n            if not demo_keys:\n                continue\n\n            obs_keys = list(f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs\"].keys())\n            img_key = None\n            for candidate in [\"agentview_rgb\", \"agentview_image\", \"image\", \"rgb\"]:\n                if candidate in obs_keys:\n                    img_key = candidate\n                    break\n            if img_key is None:\n                for k in obs_keys:\n                    shape = f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs/{k}\"].shape\n                    if len(shape) == 4 and shape[-1] == 3:\n                        img_key = k\n                        break\n            if img_key is None:\n                continue\n\n            frame_pool = []\n            for dk in demo_keys:\n                n_frames = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"].shape[0]\n                frame_pool.append((dk, n_frames))\n            total_frames = sum(n for _, n in frame_pool)\n\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n            target = min(IMAGES_PER_TASK, total_frames)\n            indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, replace=False)\n            chosen.sort()\n\n            by_demo = {}\n            for idx in chosen:\n                dk, frame_i = indices[idx]\n                by_demo.setdefault(dk, []).append(frame_i)\n\n            sampled = 0\n            for dk, frame_indices in by_demo.items():\n                imgs = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"][:]\n                for fi in frame_indices:\n                    all_images.append(Image.fromarray(imgs[fi]))\n                    task_names.append(task_name)\n                    sampled += 1\n\n            print(f\"  {task_name}: {sampled} frames\")\n\n    unique_tasks = sorted(set(task_names))\n    print(f\"[*] Total: {len(all_images)} images from {len(unique_tasks)} tasks\")\n    return all_images, task_names, unique_tasks\n\n\ndef load_model():\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Loading OpenVLA-OFT from: {CHECKPOINT_PATH}\", flush=True)\n\n    # Add the checkpoint dir to path so trust_remote_code can find the modules\n    sys.path.insert(0, CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoConfig\n\n    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        CHECKPOINT_PATH,\n        config=config,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n        attn_implementation=\"eager\",\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n    ).to(DEVICE)\n    model.eval()\n\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n    # Load processor components\n    proc_module = __import__(\"processing_prismatic\", fromlist=[\"PrismaticImageProcessor\", \"PrismaticProcessor\"])\n    PrismaticImageProcessor = proc_module.PrismaticImageProcessor\n    PrismaticProcessor = proc_module.PrismaticProcessor\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    image_processor = PrismaticImageProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    processor = PrismaticProcessor(image_processor=image_processor, tokenizer=tokenizer)\n\n    print(f\"[*] Model loaded, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\", flush=True)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_bits(p, eps=1e-10):\n    p = p.clamp(min=eps)\n    return -(p * p.log2()).sum(dim=-1)\n\n\nclass ProjectorHook:\n    \"\"\"Hook to capture and optionally prune projector output.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        self.projected_features = None\n        self.norms = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.projected_features = output.detach().clone()\n        self.norms = output.detach().norm(dim=-1)  # (B, N)\n\n        if self.keep_ratio < 1.0:\n            B, N, D = output.shape\n            k = max(1, int(N * self.keep_ratio))\n            _, topk_idx = torch.topk(self.norms, k, dim=-1)\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=output.device, dtype=output.dtype)\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\n            output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n    n_images = len(images)\n    n_layers = model.language_model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    n_heads = model.language_model.config.num_attention_heads\n    print(f\"[*] LLM: {n_layers} layers, {n_heads} heads\", flush=True)\n\n    # Register projector hook\n    hook = ProjectorHook()\n    handle = model.projector.register_forward_hook(hook)\n\n    # === Part A: Attention Entropy ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part A: Attention Entropy Analysis\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    all_entropies = []\n    all_vision_attn_frac = []\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_attentions=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n\n        attentions = outputs.attentions\n\n        if idx == 0:\n            seq_len = attentions[0].shape[-1]\n            text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n            print(f\"[*] Seq len: {seq_len}, vision: [1, {1+N_VISION_TOKENS}), text starts: {text_start}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Attention shape: {attentions[0].shape}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Layers returned: {len(attentions)}\", flush=True)\n\n        text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n        sample_entropies = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n        sample_vis_frac = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n\n        for layer_idx in range(len(attentions)):\n            attn = attentions[layer_idx]\n            text_to_vision = attn[0, :, text_start:, 1:1+N_VISION_TOKENS].float()\n            text_to_all = attn[0, :, text_start:, :].float()\n\n            vis_frac = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1) / text_to_all.sum(dim=-1).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            sample_vis_frac[layer_idx] = vis_frac.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n            vis_sum = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            text_to_vision_norm = text_to_vision / vis_sum\n\n            ent = compute_entropy_bits(text_to_vision_norm)\n            sample_entropies[layer_idx] = ent.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n        all_entropies.append(sample_entropies)\n        all_vision_attn_frac.append(sample_vis_frac)\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            cur_mean = np.mean(all_entropies)\n            cur_ratio = cur_mean / math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] entropy_ratio={cur_ratio:.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n        del outputs, attentions\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    all_entropies = np.array(all_entropies)\n    all_vision_attn_frac = np.array(all_vision_attn_frac)\n    max_entropy = math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n\n    per_layer_mean = all_entropies.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_std = all_entropies.std(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_ratio = per_layer_mean / max_entropy\n    per_layer_ratio_std = per_layer_std / max_entropy\n    per_layer_vis_frac = all_vision_attn_frac.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n\n    overall_mean = float(all_entropies.mean())\n    overall_std = float(all_entropies.std())\n    overall_ratio = overall_mean / max_entropy\n    overall_ratio_std = overall_std / max_entropy\n\n    min_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmin())\n    max_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmax())\n\n    print(f\"\\n[ENTROPY RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"Overall entropy ratio: {overall_ratio:.4f} +/- {overall_ratio_std:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Min layer: {min_layer} (ratio={per_layer_ratio[min_layer]:.4f})\")\n    print(f\"Max layer: {max_layer} (ratio={per_layer_ratio[max_layer]:.4f})\")\n\n    entropy_results = {\n        \"model\": \"OpenVLA-OFT-7B-libero-object\",\n        \"dataset\": \"LIBERO-Object\",\n        \"n_images\": n_images,\n {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio_std[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_ratio_std))},\n        \"per_layer_vision_attention_fraction\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_vis_frac[i]), 4) for i in \"overall_vision_attention_fraction\": round(float(all_vision_attn_frac.mean()), 4),\n        \"min_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": min_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[min_layer]), 4)},\n B: Pruning Curve (L2-norm, hidden state degradation)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_losses = {k: [] for k in KEEP_RATIOS}\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        # Full forward (k=1.0) to get reference hidden states\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_full = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_hidden_states=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n        h_full = out_full.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()  # text positions only\n        h_full_norm_sq = (h_full ** 2).sum().item()\n\n        pruning_losses[1.0].append(0.0)\n\n        for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n            if k >= 1.0:\n                continue\n            hook.keep_ratio = k\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                out_pruned = model(\n                    **inputs,\n                    output_hidden_states=True,\n                    return_dict=True,\n                )\n            h_pruned = out_pruned.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()\n            degradation = ((h_pruned - h_full) ** 2).sum().item()\n            normalized = degradation / max(h_full_norm_sq, 1e-10)\n            pruning_losses[k].append(normalized)\n\n            del out_pruned\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        del out_full\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 pruning_results = {}\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        vals = pruning_losses[k]\n        pruning_results[str(k)] = {\n            \"normalized_loss\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 6),\n            \"std\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 6),\n            \"n_tokens_kept\": max(1, int(N_VISION_TOKENS * k)),\n        }\n\n    print(f\"\\n[PRUNING RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"{'k':>6} {'tokens':>6} {'norm_loss':>12} {'std':>10}\")\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        r = pruning_results[str(k)]\n        print(f\"  {k:.2f}   {r['n_tokens_kept']:>4}   {r['normalized_loss']:>10.6f}   {r['std']:>8.6f}\")\n\n    handle.remove()\n\n    return entropy_results, pruning_results\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"OpenVLA-OFT Token Importance Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TA9AYH4zTD1D6xPM9WNVK9","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"\n2\tOpenVLA-OFT Token Importance Analysis: Pruning Curve + Attention Entropy.\n3\t\n4\tRuns on REAL LIBERO-Object observations. Measures:\n5\t  A) Pruning curve: L2-norm based vision token pruning → hidden state degradation\n6\t  B) Attention entropy per layer (text→vision attention distribution)\n7\t\n8\tUsage:\n9\t    source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n10\t    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python analyze_openvla_oft.py\n11\t\"\"\"\n12\t\n13\timport glob\n14\timport json\n15\timport math\n16\timport os\n17\timport sys\n18\timport time\n19\t\n20\tos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\n21\tos.environ[\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n22\tos.environ[\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n23\t\n24\timport h5py\n25\timport numpy as np\n26\timport torch\n27\tfrom PIL import Image\n28\t\n29\t# Use base OpenVLA code (OFT's modeling_prismatic.py has extra deps on prismatic package)\n30\tBASE_MODEL_CODE_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-object\"\n31\tsys.path.insert(0, BASE_MODEL_CODE_DIR)\n32\tfrom output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n190\t\n191\t        return output\n192\t\n193\t\n194\tdef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n195\t    n_images = keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-10)\n249\t            text_to_vision_norm = text_to_vision / vis_sum\n250\t\n251\t            ent = compute_entropy_bits(text_to_vision_norm)\n252\t            sample_entropies[layer_idx] = ent.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n253\t\n254\t        all_entropies.append(sample_entropies)\n255\t        all_vision_attn_frac.append(sample_vis_frac)\n256\t\n257\t [{idx+1}/{n_images}] entropy_ratio={cur_ratio:.4f}\", flush=True)\n261\t\n262\t        del outputs, attentions\n263\t        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n264\t\n265\t    all_entropies =          [k for k in f[\"data\"].keys() if k.startswith(\"demo\")],\n                key=lambda x: int(x.split(\"_\")[-1])\n            )\n            if not demo_keys:\n                continue\n\n            obs_keys = list(f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs\"].keys())\n            img_key = None\n            for candidate in [\"agentview_rgb\", \"agentview_image\", \"image\", \"rgb\"]:\n                if candidate in obs_keys:\n                    img_key = candidate\n                    break\n            if img_key is None:\n                for k in obs_keys:\n                    shape = f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs/{k}\"].shape\n                    if len(shape) == 4 and shape[-1] == 3:\n                        img_key = k\n                        break\n            if img_key is None:\n                continue\n\n            frame_pool = []\n            for dk in demo_keys:\n                n_frames = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"].shape[0]\n                frame_pool.append((dk, n_frames))\n            total_frames = sum(n for _, n in frame_pool)\n\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n            target = min(IMAGES_PER_TASK, total_frames)\n            indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, replace=False)\n            chosen.sort()\n\n            by_demo = {}\n            for idx in chosen:\n                dk, frame_i = indices[idx]\n                by_demo.setdefault(dk, []).append(frame_i)\n\n            sampled = 0\n            for dk, frame_indices in by_demo.items():\n                imgs = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"][:]\n                for fi in frame_indices:\n                    all_images.append(Image.fromarray(imgs[fi]))\n                    task_names.append(task_name)\n                    sampled += 1\n\n            print(f\"  {task_name}: {sampled} frames\")\n\n    unique_tasks = sorted(set(task_names))\n    print(f\"[*] Total: {len(all_images)} images from {len(unique_tasks)} tasks\")\n    return all_images, task_names, unique_tasks\n\n\ndef load_model():\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Loading OpenVLA-OFT from: {CHECKPOINT_PATH}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"[*] Using base model code from: {BASE_MODEL_CODE_DIR}\", flush=True)\n\n    config = OpenVLAConfig.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    model = OpenVLAForActionPrediction.from_pretrained(\n        CHECKPOINT_PATH,\n        config=config,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n        attn_implementation=\"eager\",\n    ).to(DEVICE)\n\n    with open(os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \"dataset_statistics.json\")) as f:\n        model.norm_stats = json.load(f)\n    model.eval()\n\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    image_processor = PrismaticImageProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    processor = PrismaticProcessor(image_processor=image_processor, tokenizer=tokenizer)\n\n    print(f\"[*] Model loaded, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\", flush=True)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_bits(p, eps=1e-10):\n    p = p.clamp(min=eps)\n    return -(p * p.log2()).sum(dim=-1)\n\n\nclass ProjectorHook:\n    \"\"\"Hook to capture and optionally prune projector output.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        self.projected_features = None\n        self.norms = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.projected_features = output.detach().clone()\n        self.norms = output.detach().norm(dim=-1)  # (B, N)\n\n        if self.keep_ratio < 1.0:\n            B, N, D = output.shape\n            k = max(1, int(N * self.keep_ratio))\n            _, topk_idx = torch.topk(self.norms, k, dim=-1)\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=output.device, dtype=output.dtype)\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\n            output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n    n_images = len(images)\n    n_layers = model.language_model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    n_heads = model.language_model.config.num_attention_heads\n    print(f\"[*] LLM: {n_layers} layers, {n_heads} heads\", flush=True)\n\n    # Register projector hook\n    hook = ProjectorHook()\n    handle = model.projector.register_forward_hook(hook)\n\n    # === Part A: Attention Entropy ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part A: Attention Entropy Analysis\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    all_entropies = []\n    all_vision_attn_frac = []\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_attentions=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n\n        attentions = outputs.attentions\n\n        if idx == 0:\n            seq_len = attentions[0].shape[-1]\n            text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n            print(f\"[*] Seq len: {seq_len}, vision: [1, {1+N_VISION_TOKENS}), text starts: {text_start}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Attention shape: {attentions[0].shape}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Layers returned: {len(attentions)}\", flush=True)\n\n        text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n        sample_entropies = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n        sample_vis_frac = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n\n        for layer_idx in range(len(attentions)):\n            attn = attentions[layer_idx]\n            text_to_vision = attn[0, :, text_start:, 1:1+N_VISION_TOKENS].float()\n            text_to_all = attn[0, :, text_start:, :].float()\n\n            vis_frac = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1) / text_to_all.sum(dim=-1).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            sample_vis_frac[layer_idx] = vis_frac.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n            vis_sum = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            text_to_vision_norm = text_to_vision / vis_sum\n\n            ent = compute_entropy_bits(text_to_vision_norm)\n            sample_entropies[layer_idx] = ent.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n        all_entropies.append(sample_entropies)\n        all_vision_attn_frac.append(sample_vis_frac)\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            cur_mean = np.mean(all_entropies)\n            cur_ratio = cur_mean / math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] entropy_ratio={cur_ratio:.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n        del outputs, attentions\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    all_entropies = np.array(all_entropies)\n    all_vision_attn_frac = np.array(all_vision_attn_frac)\n    max_entropy = math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n\n    per_layer_mean = all_entropies.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_std = all_entropies.std(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_ratio = per_layer_mean / max_entropy\n    per_layer_ratio_std = per_layer_std / max_entropy\n    per_layer_vis_frac = all_vision_attn_frac.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n\n    overall_mean = float(all_entropies.mean())\n    overall_std = float(all_entropies.std())\n    overall_ratio = overall_mean / max_entropy\n    overall_ratio_std = overall_std / max_entropy\n\n    min_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmin())\n    max_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmax())\n\n    print(f\"\\n[ENTROPY RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"Overall entropy ratio: {overall_ratio:.4f} +/- {overall_ratio_std:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Min layer: {min_layer} (ratio={per_layer_ratio[min_layer]:.4f})\")\n    print(f\"Max layer: {max_layer} (ratio={per_layer_ratio[max_layer]:.4f})\")\n\n    entropy_results = {\n        \"model\": \"OpenVLA-OFT-7B-libero-object\",\n        \"dataset\": \"LIBERO-Object\",\n        \"n_images\": n_images,\n        \"n_tasks_covered\": len(unique_tasks),\n        \"tasks\": unique_tasks,\n        \"n_vision_tokens\": N_VISION_TOKENS,\n        \"max_entropy_bits\": max_entropy,\n        \"n_layers\": n_layers,\n        \"n_heads\": n_heads,\n        \"per_layer_entropy_ratio\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_ratio))},\n        \"per_layer_entropy_std\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio_std[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_ratio_std))},\n        \"per_layer_vision_attention_fraction\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_vis_frac[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_vis_frac))},\n        \"overall_entropy_ratio\": round(overall_ratio, 4),\n        \"overall_entropy_std\": round(overall_ratio_std, 4),\n        \"overall_vision_attention_fraction\": round(float(all_vision_attn_frac.mean()), 4),\n        \"min_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": min_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[min_layer]), 4)},\n        \"max_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": max_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[max_layer]), 4)},\n    }\n\n    # === Part B: Pruning Curve ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part B: Pruning Curve (L2-norm, hidden state degradation)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_losses = {k: [] for k in KEEP_RATIOS}\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        # Full forward (k=1.0) to get reference hidden states\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_full = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_hidden_states=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n        h_full = out_full.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()  # text positions only\n        h_full_norm_sq = (h_full ** 2).sum().item()\n\n        pruning_losses[1.0].append(0.0)\n\n        for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n            if k >= 1.0:\n                continue\n            hook.keep_ratio = k\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                out_pruned = model(\n                    **inputs,\n                    output_hidden_states=True,\n                    return_dict=True,\n                )\n            h_pruned = out_pruned.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()\n            degradation = ((h_pruned - h_full) ** 2).sum().item()\n            normalized = degradation / max(h_full_norm_sq, 1e-10)\n            pruning_losses[k].append(normalized)\n\n            del out_pruned\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        del out_full\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            summary = {k: f\"{np.mean(v):.6f}\" for k, v in pruning_losses.items() if v}\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] losses={summary}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_results = {}\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        vals = pruning_losses[k]\n        pruning_results[str(k)] = {\n            \"normalized_loss\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 6),\n            \"std\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 6),\n            \"n_tokens_kept\": max(1, int(N_VISION_TOKENS * k)),\n        }\n\n    print(f\"\\n[PRUNING RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"{'k':>6} {'tokens':>6} {'norm_loss':>12} {'std':>10}\")\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        r = pruning_results[str(k)]\n        print(f\"  {k:.2f}   {r['n_tokens_kept']:>4}   {r['normalized_loss']:>10.6f}   {r['std']:>8.6f}\")\n\n    handle.remove()\n\n    return entropy_results, pruning_results\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"OpenVLA-OFT Token Importance Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    images, task_names, unique_tasks = load_libero_images()\n    if not images:\n        print(\"ERROR: No images loaded!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    model, processor = load_model()\n    entropy_results, pruning_results = run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks)\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n    entropy_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"openvla_oft_attention_entropy_perlayer.json\")\n    with open(entropy_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(entropy_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Entropy results saved to {entropy_path}\")\n\n    pruning_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"openvla_oft_pruning_curve.json\")\n    pruning_out = {\n        \"model\": \"OpenVLA-OFT-7B-libero-object\",\n        \"dataset\": \"LIBERO-Object\",\n        \"n_images\": len(images),\n        \"metric\": \"hidden_state_degradation_normalized\",\n        \"description\": \"MSE(H_pruned - H_full) / ||H_full||^2 at text token positions\",\n        \"keep_ratios\": {str(k): pruning_results[str(k)] for k in KEEP_RATIOS},\n    }\n    with open(pruning_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(pruning_out, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"[*] Pruning results saved to {pruning_path}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Total time: {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","numLines":418,"startLine":1,"totalLines":418}}}

return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {desc}?\\nOut:\"\n    return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {DEFAULT_PROMPT}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef load_libero_images():\n    hdf5_files =         indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, vis_sum = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            text_to_vision_norm = text_to_vision / vis_sum\n\n            ent = compute_entropy_bits(text_to_vision_norm)\n \"/root/autodl-tmp/libero_data/datasets/libero_object\"\nN_VISION_TOKENS = 256\nIMAGES_PER_TASK = 10\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts\"\n\nKEEP_RATIOS = [0.1, \"pick_up_the_milk\": \"pick up the milk and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_orange_juice\": \"pick up the orange juice and place it in the basket\",\n}\nDEFAULT_PROMPT = \"pick up the object and place it in the basket\"\n\n\ndef get_prompt_for_task(task_name):\n    tn_lower = task_name.lower()\n    for key, desc in TASK_PROMPTS.items():\n        if key in tn_lower:\n            return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {desc}?\\nOut:\"\n    return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {DEFAULT_PROMPT}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef load_libero_images():\n    hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"*.hdf5\")))\n    if not hdf5_files:\n        hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"**/*.hdf5\"), recursive=True))\n    print(f\"[*] Found {len(hdf5_files)} HDF5 files in {LIBERO_DIR}\")\n\n    all_images = []\n    task_names = []\n\n    for hdf5_path in hdf5_files:\n        task_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(hdf5_path))[0]\n\n        with h5py.File(hdf5_path, \"r\") as f:\n            if \"data\" not in f:\n                continue\n            demo_keys = sorted(\n                [k for k in f[\"data\"].keys() if k.startswith(\"demo\")],\n                key=lambda x: int(x.split(\"_\")[-1])\n            )\n            if not demo_keys:\n                continue\n\n            obs_keys = list(f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs\"].keys())\n            img_key = None\n            for candidate in [\"agentview_rgb\", \"agentview_image\", \"image\", \"rgb\"]:\n                if candidate in obs_keys:\n                    img_key = candidate\n                    break\n            if img_key is None:\n                for k in obs_keys:\n                    shape = f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs/{k}\"].shape\n                    if len(shape) == 4 and shape[-1] == 3:\n                        img_key = k\n                        break\n            if img_key is None:\n                continue\n\n            frame_pool = []\n            for dk in demo_keys:\n                n_frames = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"].shape[0]\n                frame_pool.append((dk, n_frames))\n            total_frames = sum(n for _, n in frame_pool)\n\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n            target = min(IMAGES_PER_TASK, total_frames)\n            indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, replace=False)\n            chosen.sort()\n\n            by_demo = {}\n            for idx in chosen:\n                dk, frame_i = indices[idx]\n                by_demo.setdefault(dk, []).append(frame_i)\n\n            sampled = 0\n            for dk, frame_indices in by_demo.items():\n                imgs = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"][:]\n                for fi in frame_indices:\n                    all_images.append(Image.fromarray(imgs[fi]))\n                    task_names.append(task_name)\n                    sampled += 1\n\n            print(f\"  {task_name}: {sampled} frames\")\n\n    unique_tasks = sorted(set(task_names))\n    print(f\"[*] Total: {len(all_images)} images from {len(unique_tasks)} tasks\")\n    return all_images, task_names, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n        attn_implementation=\"eager\",\n    ).to(DEVICE)\n\n    with open(os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \"dataset_statistics.json\")) as f:\n        model.norm_stats = json.load(f)\n    model.eval()\n\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    image_processor = PrismaticImageProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    processor = PrismaticProcessor(image_processor=image_processor, tokenizer=tokenizer)\n\n    print(f\"[*] Model loaded, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\", flush=True)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_bits(p, eps=1e-10):\n    p = p.clamp(min=eps)\n    return -(p * p.log2()).sum(dim=-1)\n\n\nclass ProjectorHook:\n    \"\"\"Hook to capture and optionally prune projector output.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        self.projected_features = None\n        self.norms = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.projected_features = output.detach().clone()\n        self.norms = output.detach().norm(dim=-1)  # (B, N)\n\n        if self.keep_ratio < 1.0:\n            B, N, D = output.shape\n            k = max(1, int(N * self.keep_ratio))\n            _, topk_idx = torch.topk(self.norms, k, dim=-1)\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=output.device, dtype=output.dtype)\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\n            output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n    n_images = len(images)\n    n_layers = model.language_model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    n_heads = model.language_model.config.num_attention_heads\n    print(f\"[*] LLM: {n_layers} layers, {n_heads} heads\", flush=True)\n\n    # Register projector hook\n    hook = ProjectorHook()\n    handle = model.projector.register_forward_hook(hook)\n\n    # === Part A: Attention Entropy ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part A: = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_attentions=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n\n        attentions = outputs.attentions\n\n        if idx == 0:\n            seq_len = attentions[0].shape[-1]\n            text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n            print(f\"[*] Seq len: {seq_len}, vision: [1, {1+N_VISION_TOKENS}), text starts: {text_start}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Attention shape: {attentions[0].shape}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Layers returned: {len(attentions)}\", flush=True)\n\n ProjectorHook:\n174\t    \"\"\"Hook to capture and optionally prune projector output.\"\"\"\n175\t    def __init__(self):\n176\t        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n177\t        self.projected_features = return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {desc}?\\nOut:\"\n    return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {DEFAULT_PROMPT}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef load_libero_images():\n    hdf5_files = {len(hdf5_files)} HDF5 files in {LIBERO_DIR}\")\n\n    all_images = []\n    task_names = []\n\n    for hdf5_path in hdf5_files:\n        task_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(hdf5_path))[0]\n\n        with h5py.File(hdf5_path, \"r\") as f:\n            if \"data\" not in f:\n                continue\n            demo_keys = sorted(\n                [k for k in f[\"data\"].keys() if k.startswith(\"demo\")],\n                key=lambda x: int(x.split(\"_\")[-1])\n            )\n            if not demo_keys:\n                continue\n\n            obs_keys = list(f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs\"].keys())\n            img_key = None\n            for candidate in [\"agentview_rgb\", \"agentview_image\", \"image\", \"rgb\"]:\n                if candidate in obs_keys:\n                    img_key = candidate\n                    break\n            if img_key is None:\n                for k in obs_keys:\n                    shape = f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs/{k}\"].shape\n                    if len(shape) == 4 and shape[-1] == 3:\n                        img_key = k\n                        break\n            if img_key is None:\n                continue\n\n            frame_pool = []\n            for dk in demo_keys:\n                n_frames = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"].shape[0]\n                frame_pool.append((dk, n_frames))\n            total_frames = sum(n for _, n in frame_pool)\n\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n            target = min(IMAGES_PER_TASK, total_frames)\n            indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, replace=False)\n            chosen.sort()\n\n            by_demo = {}\n            for idx in chosen:\n                dk, frame_i = indices[idx]\n                by_demo.setdefault(dk, []).append(frame_i)\n\n            sampled = 0\n            for dk, frame_indices in by_demo.items():\n                imgs = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"][:]\n                for fi in frame_indices:\n                    all_images.append(Image.fromarray(imgs[fi]))\n                    task_names.append(task_name)\n                    sampled += 1\n\n            print(f\"  {task_name}: {sampled} frames\")\n\n    unique_tasks = sorted(set(task_names))\n    print(f\"[*] Total: {len(all_images)} images from {len(unique_tasks)} tasks\")\n    return all_images, task_names, unique_tasks\n\n\ndef load_model():\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Loading OpenVLA-OFT from: {CHECKPOINT_PATH}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"[*] Using base model code from: {BASE_MODEL_CODE_DIR}\", flush=True)\n\n    config = OpenVLAConfig.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    model = OpenVLAForActionPrediction.from_pretrained(\n        CHECKPOINT_PATH,\n        config=config,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n        attn_implementation=\"eager\",\n    ).to(DEVICE)\n\n    with open(os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \"dataset_statistics.json\")) as f:\n        model.norm_stats = json.load(f)\n    model.eval()\n\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    image_processor = PrismaticImageProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    processor = PrismaticProcessor(image_processor=image_processor, tokenizer=tokenizer)\n\n    print(f\"[*] Model loaded, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\", flush=True)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_bits(p, eps=1e-10):\n    p = p.clamp(min=eps)\n    return -(p * p.log2()).sum(dim=-1)\n\n\nclass ProjectorHook:\n    \"\"\"Hook to capture and optionally prune projector output.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        self.projected_features = None\n        self.norms = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.projected_features = output.detach().clone()\n        self.norms = output.detach().norm(dim=-1)  # (B, N)\n\n        if self.keep_ratio < 1.0:\n            B, N, D = output.shape\n            k = max(1, int(N * self.keep_ratio))\n            _, topk_idx = torch.topk(self.norms, k, dim=-1)\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=output.device, dtype=output.dtype)\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\n            output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n    n_images = len(images)\n    n_layers = model.language_model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    n_heads = model.language_model.config.num_attention_heads\n    print(f\"[*] LLM: {n_layers} layers, {n_heads} heads\", flush=True)\n\n    # Register projector hook\n    hook = ProjectorHook()\n    handle = model.projector.register_forward_hook(hook)\n\n    # === Part A: Attention Entropy ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part A: = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_attentions=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n\n        attentions = outputs.attentions\n\n        if idx == 0:\n            seq_len = attentions[0].shape[-1]\n            text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n            print(f\"[*] Seq len: {seq_len}, vision: [1, {1+N_VISION_TOKENS}), text starts: {text_start}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Attention shape: {attentions[0].shape}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Layers returned: {len(attentions)}\", flush=True)\n\n        text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n        sample_entropies = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n        sample_vis_frac = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n\n        for layer_idx in range(len(attentions)):\n            attn = attentions[layer_idx]\n            text_to_vision = attn[0, :, text_start:, 1:1+N_VISION_TOKENS].float()\n            text_to_all = attn[0, :, text_start:, :].float()\n\n            vis_frac = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1) / text_to_all.sum(dim=-1).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            sample_vis_frac[layer_idx] = vis_frac.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n            vis_sum = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            text_to_vision_norm = text_to_vision / vis_sum\n\n            ent = compute_entropy_bits(text_to_vision_norm)\n            sample_entropies[layer_idx] = ent.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n        all_entropies.append(sample_entropies)\n        all_vision_attn_frac.append(sample_vis_frac)\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            cur_mean = np.mean(all_entropies)\n            cur_ratio = cur_mean / math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] entropy_ratio={cur_ratio:.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n        del outputs, attentions\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    all_entropies = np.array(all_entropies)\n    all_vision_attn_frac = np.array(all_vision_attn_frac)\n    max_entropy = math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n\n    per_layer_mean = all_entropies.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_std = all_entropies.std(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_ratio = per_layer_mean / max_entropy\n    per_layer_ratio_std = per_layer_std / max_entropy\n    per_layer_vis_frac = all_vision_attn_frac.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n\n    overall_mean = float(all_entropies.mean())\n    overall_std = float(all_entropies.std())\n    overall_ratio = overall_mean / max_entropy\n    overall_ratio_std = overall_std / max_entropy\n\n    min_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmin())\n    max_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmax())\n\n    print(f\"\\n[ENTROPY RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"Overall entropy ratio: {overall_ratio:.4f} +/- \"overall_entropy_ratio\": round(overall_ratio, 4),\n        \"overall_entropy_std\": round(overall_ratio_std, 4),\n        \"overall_vision_attention_fraction\": hidden state degradation)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_losses = {k: [] for k in KEEP_RATIOS}\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        # Full forward (k=1.0) to get reference hidden states\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_full = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_hidden_states=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n        h_full = out_full.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()  # text positions only\n        h_full_norm_sq = (h_full ** 2).sum().item()\n\n        pruning_losses[1.0].append(0.0)\n\n        for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n            if k >= 1.0:\n                continue\n            hook.keep_ratio = k\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                out_pruned = model(\n                    **inputs,\n                    output_hidden_states=True,\n                    return_dict=True,\n                )\n            h_pruned = out_pruned.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()\n            degradation = ((h_pruned - h_full) ** 2).sum().item()\n            normalized = degradation / max(h_full_norm_sq, 1e-10)\n            pruning_losses[k].append(normalized)\n\n            del out_pruned\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        del out_full\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            summary = {k: f\"{np.mean(v):.6f}\" for k, v in pruning_losses.items() if v}\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] losses={summary}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_results = {}\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        vals = pruning_losses[k]\n        pruning_results[str(k)] = {\n            \"normalized_loss\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 6),\n            \"std\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 6),\n            \"n_tokens_kept\": max(1, int(N_VISION_TOKENS * k)),\n        }\n\n    print(f\"\\n[PRUNING RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"{'k':>6} {'tokens':>6} {'norm_loss':>12} {'std':>10}\")\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        r = pruning_results[str(k)]\n        print(f\"  {k:.2f}   {r['n_tokens_kept']:>4}   {r['normalized_loss']:>10.6f}   {r['std']:>8.6f}\")\n\n    handle.remove()\n\n    return entropy_results, pruning_results\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"OpenVLA-OFT Token Importance Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" in KEEP_RATIOS},\n    }\n    with open(pruning_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(pruning_out, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"[*] Pruning results saved to {pruning_path}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Total f\"In: What action should the robot take to {DEFAULT_PROMPT}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef load_libero_images():\n    hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"*.hdf5\")))\n    if not hdf5_files:\n        hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"**/*.hdf5\"), recursive=True))\n    print(f\"[*] Found {len(hdf5_files)} HDF5 files in {LIBERO_DIR}\")\n\n    all_images = []\n    task_names = []\n\n    for hdf5_path in hdf5_files:\n        task_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(hdf5_path))[0]\n\n        with h5py.File(hdf5_path, \"r\") as f:\n            if \"data\" not in f:\n                continue\n            demo_keys = sorted(\n                [k for k in f[\"data\"].keys() if k.startswith(\"demo\")],\n                key=lambda x: int(x.split(\"_\")[-1])\n            )\n            if not demo_keys:\n                continue\n\n            obs_keys = list(f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs\"].keys())\n            img_key = None\n            for candidate in [\"agentview_rgb\", \"agentview_image\", \"image\", \"rgb\"]:\n                if candidate in obs_keys:\n                    img_key = candidate\n                    break\n            if img_key is None:\n                for k in obs_keys:\n                    shape = f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs/{k}\"].shape\n                    if len(shape) == 4 and shape[-1] == 3:\n                        img_key = k\n                        break\n            if img_key is None:\n                continue\n\n            frame_pool = []\n            for dk in demo_keys:\n                n_frames = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"].shape[0]\n                frame_pool.append((dk, n_frames))\n            total_frames = sum(n for _, n in frame_pool)\n\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n            target = min(IMAGES_PER_TASK, total_frames)\n            indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, replace=False)\n            chosen.sort()\n\n            by_demo = {}\n            for idx in chosen:\n                dk, frame_i = indices[idx]\n                by_demo.setdefault(dk, []).append(frame_i)\n\n            sampled = 0\n            for dk, frame_indices in by_demo.items():\n                imgs = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"][:]\n                for fi in frame_indices:\n                    all_images.append(Image.fromarray(imgs[fi]))\n                    task_names.append(task_name)\n                    sampled += 1\n\n            print(f\"  {task_name}: {sampled} frames\")\n\n    unique_tasks = sorted(set(task_names))\n    print(f\"[*] Total: {len(all_images)} images from base model code from: {BASE_MODEL_CODE_DIR}\", flush=True)\n\n    config = OpenVLAConfig.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    model = OpenVLAForActionPrediction.from_pretrained(\n open(os.path.join(CHECKPOINT_PATH, \"dataset_statistics.json\")) as f:\n        model.norm_stats = json.load(f)\n    model.eval()\n\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    image_processor = PrismaticImageProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    processor = PrismaticProcessor(image_processor=image_processor, tokenizer=tokenizer)\n\n    print(f\"[*] Model loaded, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\", flush=True)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_bits(p, eps=1e-10):\n    p = p.clamp(min=eps)\n    return -(p * p.log2()).sum(dim=-1)\n\n\nclass ProjectorHook:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        self.projected_features = None\n        self.norms = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.projected_features = output.detach().clone()\n        self.norms = output.detach().norm(dim=-1)\n\n        if self.keep_ratio < 1.0:\n            B, N, D = output.shape\n            k = max(1, int(N * self.keep_ratio))\n            _, topk_idx = torch.topk(self.norms, k, dim=-1)\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=output.device, dtype=output.dtype)\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\n            output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n    n_images = len(images)\n    n_layers = model.language_model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    n_heads = model.projector.register_forward_hook(hook)\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part A: Attention Entropy Analysis\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    all_entropies = []\n    all_vision_attn_frac = []\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n\n        inputs = output_attentions=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n\n        attentions = outputs.attentions\n\n        if idx == 0:\n            seq_len = attentions[0].shape[-1]\n            text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n            print(f\"[*] Seq len: {seq_len}, vision: [1, {1+N_VISION_TOKENS}), text starts: {text_start}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Attention shape: {attentions[0].shape}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Layers returned: {len(attentions)}\", flush=True)\n\n        text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n        sample_entropies = np.zeros((n_layers, text_to_all.sum(dim=-1).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            sample_vis_frac[layer_idx] = vis_frac.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n            vis_sum = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1, del outputs, attentions\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    all_entropies = np.array(all_entropies)\n    all_vision_attn_frac = np.array(all_vision_attn_frac)\n    max_entropy = per_layer_ratio_std = per_layer_std / max_entropy\n    per_layer_vis_frac = all_vision_attn_frac.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n\n    overall_mean = float(all_entropies.mean())\n    overall_std = float(all_entropies.std())\n    overall_ratio = overall_mean / max_entropy\n    overall_ratio_std = overall_std / max_entropy\n\n    min_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmin())\n    max_layer =    \"max_entropy_bits\": max_entropy,\n        \"n_layers\": n_layers,\n        \"n_heads\": n_heads,\n        \"per_layer_entropy_ratio\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio[i]), 4) for i in min_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[min_layer]), 4)},\n        \"max_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": max_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[max_layer]), 4)},\n    }\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part B: Pruning Curve (L2-norm, hidden state degradation)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_losses = {k: [] for k in KEEP_RATIOS}\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, pruning_losses[1.0].append(0.0)\n\n        for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n            if k >= 1.0:\n                continue\n            hook.keep_ratio = k\n            with torch.no_grad():\n
